# Lab genealogy 2: founders, moves and licensing-and-hiring deals, 2023–2026

Generated from `pack.json` by `node research/lab-genealogy-2/tools.mjs render`; do not edit by hand. A candidate research pack outside application data: machine-prepared and machine-audited, with no human review. Each line is one record; read its source before relying on it.

## How to read this

- A line records that a source **documents** an event or a role at a date. It does not say why anything happened. Where a person or organisation stated a reason in its own words, the record attributes it (see [Stated reasons](#stated-reasons)).
- Dates keep their precision. "as of" marks a role shown on a dated document (a snapshot), not a start date. An announcement date is not a start or last working day. "Undated" means no source gave a date.
- "(reporting)" marks a record that rests on news reporting because no primary source could be retrieved.
- `dataset record` lines are already in Selekoa's dataset and are shown here for continuity; they were not re-checked by this pack.
- A sequence of roles is not a continuous employment history, and a move is not a transfer of influence, ideas or loyalty.

## Coverage

280 new candidate records from 128 sources (61 primary, 67 reporting), shown together with 369 existing dataset records on the same labs. 104 people appear in the pack (79 proposed as new entities) and 59 organisations are proposed as new entities. 6 records carry a reason stated by the person or organisation concerned.

## Lab by lab

### Google: DeepMind, Google Brain and Google DeepMind

DeepMind, Google Brain and Google DeepMind stay distinct entities; the 2023 combination is recorded as a combination, not a legal merger of companies. Google and Alphabet are grouped here as the parent organisations, so a move between Google and one of its research units is not shown as a move between labs.

## Timeline

### 2015

- **2015– (end not stated).** Kyutai’s launch announcement of 17 November 2023 states that Hervé Jégou joined Facebook AI Research when its Paris laboratory was founded in 2015, serving as manager and then as director. _(CMA CGM Group; `lg2-eu-jegou-fair-2015`)_

### 2018

- **March 2018–June 2019.** Zhipu’s Hong Kong prospectus of 30 December 2025 states that Wang Shaolan worked at Tsinghua University from March 2018 to June 2019. _(Knowledge Atlas Technology Joint Stock Company Limited (via HKEXnews); `lg2-cn-wang-shaolan-tsinghua-2018-2019`)_

### 2023

- **2023–2024.** Andrej Karpathy describes returning to OpenAI in 2023–2024 to build a midtraining and synthetic-data team. _(dataset record; `karpathy-openai-return-2023-2024`)_
- **2023.** Moonshot AI’s “About” page dates its formal establishment to spring 2023 (“2023年 春天 … 月之暗面（Moonshot AI）正式成立”). _(dataset record; `lg-cn-moonshot-founded-2023`)_
- **as of 2023.** Jiemian reported on 21 February 2025 that Google promoted Wu Yonghui in 2023 to Google Fellow and vice president of research at Google DeepMind. _(dataset record; `lg-gc-wu-yonghui-google-deepmind-2023`)_
- **23 January 2023.** Microsoft announced a multiyear, multibillion-dollar investment in OpenAI. _(dataset record; `microsoft-investment-2023`)_
- **February 2023.** Zhipu’s Hong Kong prospectus of 30 December 2025 states that the company completed its Series B1 financing in February 2023, with a total consideration of RMB208,000,000 for the newly subscribed registered capital listed. _(Knowledge Atlas Technology Joint Stock Company Limited (via HKEXnews); `lg2-cn-zhipu-round-b1`)_
- **as of 27 February 2023.** Fortune, republishing Bloomberg, reported on 27 February 2023, attributing the information to a Meta spokesperson, that Ahmad Al-Dahle would lead Meta's new generative AI product group. _(dataset record; `lg-mx-aldahle-genai-lead-2023`)_
- **as of 27 February 2023.** Fortune, republishing Bloomberg, reported on 27 February 2023 that Mark Zuckerberg had announced in an Instagram post that Meta was creating a new top-level product group focused on generative AI. _(dataset record; `lg-mx-meta-genai-unit-2023`)_
- **as of 27 February 2023.** The first LLaMA paper, dated 27 February 2023, lists Timothée Lacroix under the Meta AI affiliation. _(dataset record; `lg-new-lacroix-meta-2023`)_
- **as of 27 February 2023.** The first LLaMA paper, dated 27 February 2023, lists Guillaume Lample under the Meta AI affiliation. _(dataset record; `lg-new-lample-meta-2023`)_
- **as of 27 February 2023.** The first LLaMA paper lists Hugo Touvron under the Meta AI affiliation. _(dataset record; `xm-touvron-meta`)_
- **24 March 2023.** Baichuan Intelligence’s website states that the company was established on 24 March 2023. _(dataset record; `lg-cn-baichuan-founded-2023`)_
- **24 March 2023.** Baichuan Intelligence’s website states that the company was founded by Wang Xiaochuan, former CEO of Sogou. _(dataset record; `lg-cn-wang-xiaochuan-baichuan-founder`)_
- **April 2023.** Kechuangban Ribao (科创板日报) reported on 23 March 2024 that StepFun was established in April 2023. _(dataset record; `lg-cn-stepfun-founded-2023`)_
- **April 2023.** Mistral AI’s company page states that Mistral was born in April 2023. _(dataset record; `lg-new-mistral-founding-2023`)_
- **20 April 2023.** The Brain team from Google Research was announced as joining DeepMind in Google DeepMind. _(dataset record; `brain-combined-2023`)_
- **as of 20 April 2023.** Google announced that Jeff Dean would become Google’s Chief Scientist in April 2023. _(dataset record; `dean-google-chief-scientist-2023`)_
- **as of 20 April 2023.** Google’s combination announcement stated that Jeff Dean would serve as Chief Scientist to Google DeepMind. _(dataset record; `dean-google-deepmind-chief-scientist-2023`)_
- **20 April 2023.** DeepMind was announced as joining the Brain team in a combined unit called Google DeepMind. _(dataset record; `deepmind-combined-2023`)_
- **as of 20 April 2023.** Demis Hassabis stated that he would lead the combined Google DeepMind unit. _(dataset record; `hassabis-deepmind-2023`)_
- **as of 20 April 2023.** The April 2023 combination announcement identifies Demis Hassabis as DeepMind CEO. _(dataset record; `hassabis-deepmind-ceo-2023`)_

- **January 2024.** Zhipu’s Hong Kong prospectus of 30 December 2025 lists Shanghai Yunya, which it describes as an investment vehicle of Ant Group, among the subscribers in its Series B3 financing, completed in January 2024. _(Knowledge Atlas Technology Joint Stock Company Limited (via HKEXnews); `lg2-cn-zhipu-ant-b3`)_

- **March 2024.** TechCrunch reported on 9 October 2025 that Reflection AI was founded the previous year and launched in March 2024 by Misha Laskin and Ioannis Antonoglou. _(TechCrunch (reporting); `lg2-nl-reflection-founding-stated-2024`)_
- **?–March 2024.** Quartz reported on 3 April 2024 that Logan Kilpatrick had been OpenAI's head of developer relations and had left OpenAI in March 2024. _(Quartz (reporting); `lg2-oa-kilpatrick-openai-devrel-prior`)_
- **as of 19 March 2024.** Microsoft announced on 19 March 2024 a new organisation called Microsoft AI, formed with Mustafa Suleyman and Karén Simonyan. _(dataset record; `lg-deal-microsoft-ai-unit-2024`)_
- **19 March 2024.** The UK Competition and Markets Authority stated in the summary of its phase 1 decision (published on 4 September 2024) that Microsoft had announced on 19 March 2024 the hiring of former Inflection employees amounting, in the CMA's understanding, to almost all of Inflection's team, and that Microsoft had entered into arrangements with Inflection including a non-exclusive licence to use Inflection IP. _(dataset record; `lg-deal-microsoft-inflection-agreement-2024`)_

- **April 2024.** Business Insider reported on 9 May 2024 that Daniel Kokotajlo, who worked on OpenAI's governance team, had left the company the previous month; Kokotajlo's LessWrong profile states that he quit OpenAI due to losing confidence that it would behave responsibly around the time of AGI. _(dataset record; `lg-gs-kokotajlo-openai-departure-2024`)_

- **as of 25 September 2024.** TechCrunch reported on 25 September 2024 that Josh Achiam, a research scientist, would take on a new role at OpenAI as head of mission alignment. _(dataset record; `lg-gs-achiam-openai-mission-alignment-2024`)_
- **25 September 2024.** TechCrunch reported on 25 September 2024 that OpenAI’s chief research officer, Bob McGrew, had left the company, according to a post on X by Sam Altman. _(dataset record; `lg-oai-mcgrew-departure-2024`)_
- **25 September 2024.** TechCrunch reported on 25 September 2024 that OpenAI’s CTO Mira Murati had posted on X that she was leaving the company. _(dataset record; `lg-oai-murati-departure-2024`)_
- **as of 26 September 2024.** Mark Chen posted on X on 26 September 2024 (UTC) that he would lead research at OpenAI alongside the account @merettm; TechCrunch reported on 25 September 2024 that Sam Altman had said Chen was being promoted to SVP of research and would lead the research organisation in partnership with Jakub Pachocki. _(dataset record; `lg-oai-markchen-research-lead-2024`)_
- **26 September 2024.** Barret Zoph posted on X on 26 September 2024 (UTC) a note to OpenAI saying that he had decided to leave the company. _(dataset record; `lg-oai-zoph-departure-2024`)_
- **as of 3 October 2024.** Tim Brooks posted on X on 3 October 2024 that he would be joining Google DeepMind to work on video generation and world simulators, after two years at OpenAI working on Sora. _(X; `lg2-oa-brooks-gdm-2024`)_
- **as of 9 October 2024.** Google DeepMind identified John Jumper as a Director in its October 2024 announcement. _(dataset record; `atlas-jumper-gdm`)_
- **14 October 2024.** Bloomberg reported on 14 October 2024 that Microsoft had said that Sébastien Bubeck, one of its artificial intelligence vice presidents, was leaving to join OpenAI; Maginative reported the same day a Microsoft spokesperson’s statement that he had decided to leave Microsoft to further his work toward developing artificial general intelligence. _(Maginative (reporting); Bloomberg (as carried by Fortune) (reporting); `lg2-oa-bubeck-microsoft-departure-2024`)_
- **as of 14 October 2024.** Bloomberg reported on 14 October 2024 that Microsoft had said that Sébastien Bubeck was leaving to join OpenAI; Maginative reported the same day that his role at OpenAI was unclear. _(Maginative (reporting); Bloomberg (as carried by Fortune) (reporting); `lg2-oa-bubeck-openai-2024`)_

- **23 October 2024.** Miles Brundage, who led OpenAI's AGI Readiness team, wrote on 23 October 2024 that the remainder of the team would be distributed among other teams. _(dataset record; `lg-gs-agi-readiness-team-distributed-2024`)_
- **23 October 2024.** Miles Brundage wrote on 23 October 2024 that he was leaving OpenAI, where he was Senior Advisor for AGI Readiness; he stated that he wanted to impact and influence AI's development from outside the industry. _(dataset record; `lg-gs-brundage-openai-departure-2024`)_
- **November 2024.** Zhipu’s Hong Kong prospectus of 30 December 2025 lists Shanghai Feiya, which it describes as wholly owned, through Accelerator VIII Ltd., by Ant Unicorn Fund, L.P., whose general partner is an indirect wholly owned subsidiary of Ant Group, among the subscribers in its Series B5 financing, completed in November 2024. _(Knowledge Atlas Technology Joint Stock Company Limited (via HKEXnews); `lg2-cn-zhipu-ant-b5`)_

- **November 2024.** Zhipu’s Hong Kong prospectus of 30 December 2025 states that the company completed its Series B5 financing in November 2024, with a total consideration of RMB1,358,934,041 for the newly subscribed registered capital listed. _(Knowledge Atlas Technology Joint Stock Company Limited (via HKEXnews); `lg2-cn-zhipu-round-b5`)_
- **8 November 2024.** Lilian Weng posted on X on 8 November 2024 that she had decided to leave OpenAI after almost seven years. _(dataset record; `lg-oai-weng-departure-2024`)_

- **22 November 2024.** Anthropic announced a new $4 billion Amazon investment, which would bring the total to $8 billion. _(dataset record; `amazon-anthropic-new-2024`)_
- **as of 25 November 2024.** Yi Tay wrote on 25 November 2024 that he was returning to Google DeepMind as a senior staff research scientist. _(dataset record; `lg-new-tay-gdm-2024`)_

- **1 December 2024.** Rosie Campbell wrote on 1 December 2024 that it had been her last week at OpenAI; she stated that, after Miles Brundage's departure and the dissolution of the AGI Readiness team, she believed she could pursue the mission of ensuring safe and beneficial AGI more effectively externally. _(dataset record; `lg-gs-campbell-openai-departure-2024`)_
- **as of 3 December 2024.** Amazon's announcement of Amazon Nova on 3 December 2024 identifies Rohit Prasad as SVP of Amazon Artificial General Intelligence. _(Amazon; `lg2-xa-prasad-amazon-svp-agi-2024`)_
- **as of 4 December 2024.** heise online reported on 4 December 2024, on the basis of an OpenAI press release, that Lucas Beyer, previously a Staff Research Scientist at Google DeepMind, was joining OpenAI's new Zurich team. _(heise online (reporting); `lg2-oa-beyer-openai-2024`)_
- **as of 4 December 2024.** heise online reported on 4 December 2024, on the basis of an OpenAI press release, that Alexander Kolesnikov, previously a Staff Research Scientist at Google DeepMind, was joining OpenAI's new Zurich team. _(heise online (reporting); `lg2-oa-kolesnikov-openai-2024`)_

- **20 December 2024.** QbitAI reported on 20 December 2024, relaying The Information, that Alec Radford, first author of the first GPT paper, was leaving OpenAI to pursue independent research, and quoted Mark Chen as saying OpenAI looked forward to continuing to collaborate with him. _(量子位 (QbitAI) (reporting); `lg2-oa-radford-openai-departure-2024`)_

## Who went where

People in this pack documented at two or more organisations in this pack and the dataset (all years), in date order. Each step is a separate record; gaps between them are unknown, undated roles are listed last, and the path is not an employment history.

### Between organisations

For each person above, each change of organisation between consecutive dated records (a lab and its units count as one organisation), with the two records. The order is that of the dates of the records, which may be snapshots or announcements: a pair is not a documented transfer, it does not say the earlier role had ended, and undated records, departure announcements and changes between records whose dates overlap (the same day, or 2024 and May 2024) are left out. ## Stated reasons

Reasons appear only where the person or organisation concerned stated them in a cited source. They are attributed statements, not findings.

- `lg2-oa-bubeck-microsoft-departure-2024` — Microsoft stated: A Microsoft spokesperson stated that Bubeck had decided to leave Microsoft to further his work toward developing artificial general intelligence. _(evidence `lg2-ev-oa-bubeck-microsoft-departure-2024`)_
- `lg2-oa-carlini-gdm-departure-2025` — Nicholas Carlini stated: Carlini wrote that he no longer believed DeepMind (and DeepMind leadership) supported the type of high-impact security and privacy research he likes to do. _(evidence `lg2-ev-oa-carlini-gdm-departure-2025-2`)_
- `lg2-gm-agarwal-leaves-meta-2025` — Rishabh Agarwal stated: Agarwal wrote that, after 7.5 years across Google Brain, DeepMind and Meta, he “felt the pull” to take on a different kind of risk. _(evidence `lg2-ev-gm-agarwal-leaves-meta-2025-reason`)_
- `lg2-oa-tworek-openai-departure-2026` — Jerry Tworek stated: As relayed by TrendForce, which cites The Verge’s report of Tworek’s X post, Tworek said he wanted to pursue research that is difficult to carry out within OpenAI. _(evidence `lg2-ev-oa-tworek-openai-departure-2026`)_
- `lg2-xa-luan-amazon-departure-2026` — David Luan stated: Luan wrote, in a LinkedIn message relayed in French translation by Next, that with artificial general intelligence so close he had decided to spend 100 percent of his time teaching AI systems entirely new capabilities (an English rendering of the French translation). _(evidence `lg2-ev-xa-luan-amazon-departure-2026-reason`)_
- `lg2-gm-velloso-leaves-meta-2026` — Mat Velloso stated: Velloso said, in an interview relayed by Baguete, that Meta was not the right place for him to influence things in the way he considered necessary. _(evidence `lg2-ev-gm-velloso-leaves-meta-2026-2`)_

## How we searched

Each drafting pass recorded what it searched and what it could not retrieve.

- **cn** ([drafts/cn.json](drafts/cn.json)): Searched 2026-10-29 for 2023–2026 founders, leaders, moves and funding rounds of Chinese frontier labs (Zhipu, MiniMax, Moonshot AI, Baichuan, 01.AI, StepFun, DeepSeek, ByteDance Seed, Alibaba Qwen/Tongyi, Tencent, Baidu, Xiaomi), after checking existing-claims.txt, existing-entities.txt (including Chinese aliases), people-pack-claims.txt and the capital records in data/claims.json (the c2 MiniMax investor records are not in existing-claims.txt). Primary sources retrieved and used: Zhipu’s English HKEX prospectus of 30 December 2025 (pre-IPO rounds B1–B6, investor descriptions, directors and senior management) and MiniMax’s English HKEX prospectus of 31 December 2025 (executive Directors), both direct from hkexnews.hk. Chinese reporting used where no primary text was retrieved: Jiemian 2025-11-12 (relaying Luo Fuli’s WeChat Moments post), National Business Daily 2025-12-17, Jiemian 2025-03-17 (relaying Lanjing News and an earlier 36Kr report with Baichuan’s reply), 36Kr 2026-07-24 (republishing a Bowang Caijing commentary; used only for dated departures and co-founder designations, not for its claims about motives), Jiemian 2025-05-09 (relaying 36Kr and 01.AI’s reply), The Paper 2026-01-26 (StepFun B+ round), Lanjing News 2025-12-31 (Moonshot internal letter). Web search was used only to discover addresses. WeChat Moments posts and internal letters cannot be retrieved; records relaying them say so. cninfo’s announcement index for Huichen (688500) was queried for June–October 2026 to check a reported appointment of Ru Liyun; no such filing was found. Note on the Zhipu prospectus: its text layer drops Chinese characters and interleaves table columns; round totals were read from rows labelled “Total” and checked by summing the listed considerations (B3’s listed rows sum to about RMB115 short of the stated total; the stated total is recorded). Chinese name forms of Zhipu and MiniMax personnel are not in the English text layer; 趙鵬宇 is taken from the Chinese MiniMax prospectus (2025123100026_c), whose text layer is otherwise mostly without CJK characters; Zhou Yucong’s Chinese name could not be read.
- **dl** ([drafts/dl.json](drafts/dl.json)): Searched on 29 October 2026 for acqui-hire and licensing-plus-hiring arrangements of 2023-2026 not yet in the dataset: Amazon-Covariant (aboutamazon.com, covariant.ai, GeekWire), Meta-Scale AI (Scale AI's announcement), regulator texts on the existing deals (gov.uk CMA case pages for Microsoft/Inflection; web searches for CMA or FTC case pages on Amazon-Adept, Google-Character.AI, Amazon-Covariant, NVIDIA-Groq), NVIDIA-Groq (groq.com newsroom, CNBC), NVIDIA-Enfabrica (CNBC), Anthropic and the Humanloop team (humanloop.com, TechCrunch), Google DeepMind and Hume AI (hume.ai blog, TechCrunch, HIT Consultant, The Decoder), and Meta's 2025 founder hires. Covariant's own blog post on the Amazon agreement could not be retrieved: covariant.ai now serves a placeholder page and the post's address returns 404, so Covariant's leadership change rests on GeekWire's report of that post. No Hume AI or Google text on the Hume agreement and no Enfabrica or NVIDIA text on the Enfabrica agreement was found; those records rest on reporting. Wired, which first reported the Hume agreement, is not reachable through the search tool and was not retrieved. No CMA or FTC case page or decision was found for Amazon-Adept, Google-Character.AI, Amazon-Covariant or NVIDIA-Groq; the FTC chair's January 2026 remarks on acqui-hires were reported from a television interview and are not a decision on a named deal.
- **eu** ([drafts/eu.json](drafts/eu.json)): Searched 29 October 2026 (web search for discovery only; every passage read from the retrieved text). Mistral AI: mistral.ai news for 2023–2026 rounds; Lightspeed and a16z posts; tech.eu and TechCrunch for the Series A and B, for which no Mistral AI text was located. Inflection AI: inflection.ai blog index (the June 2023 funding release is no longer on the site), Business Wire (refused), TechCrunch; Inflection’s May 2024 and July 2026 posts. Reid Hoffman’s later roles: only Manas AI found (a drug-discovery company; omitted). Microsoft AI: Official Microsoft Blog posts of April 2024 (London hub) and March 2026 (Copilot leadership update); microsoft.ai news and about pages (no leadership roster; no text on Karén Simonyan’s later remit). Hires from Google DeepMind to Microsoft AI (Dominic King, Amar Subramanya and others) were found only in reporting or LinkedIn posts. European and new labs founded by frontier-lab people: Latent Labs press releases; Adaption blog, Emergence Capital post, Wilson Sonsini note; Ineffable Intelligence site, Companies House register, Cooley release, Fortune; Kyutai site and the CMA CGM launch release. The r.jina.ai relay refused all requests from this environment during this pass (HTTP 401, IP reputation), so publishers that need the relay were retrieved directly where possible. Not searched: Black Forest Labs (founders from Stability AI, not a frontier lab in scope), Bioptimus, Reka (founded 2022; already recorded).
- **gm** ([drafts/gm.json](drafts/gm.json)): Searched on 29 October 2026 (web search for discovery only; every passage copied from a retrieved copy): Google DeepMind leadership appointments and departures 2023–2026 (Gemini app leadership, Chief AI Architect, the August 2026 reshuffle, departures of Oriol Vinyals, Quoc Le, David Silver and Mat Velloso, departures to found labs); Meta's 2025–2026 AI reorganisations (the May 2025 split into AI products and AGI Foundations, the August 2025 four-group MSL structure, the March 2026 applied AI engineering organisation), MSL leaders named after the 30 June 2025 memo, self-announced MSL departures, Ruoming Pang's and Andrew Tulloch's Meta roles, and 2026 departures (Aparna Ramani, Rohit Patel). Primary texts used: Google's published note of 5 August 2026, X posts by Oriol Vinyals and Rishabh Agarwal (syndication endpoint), and Google Cloud's biography of Aparna Ramani. Internal memos (Chris Cox, May 2025; Alexandr Wang, August 2025; Maher Saba, March 2026; Ramani, April 2026) are unpublished and rest on the reporting that relays them (Axios, Bloomberg via Mobile World Live/Storyboard18/TechTarget/BTW Media, the Wall Street Journal via The Decoder, The Information via WinBuzzer). The Google DeepMind About page, retrieved, lists no leadership team. Vinyals's long post is truncated in the syndication text. Quoc Le's X post (as relayed) concerns co-founding Discovery Loop, not leaving Google, so his departure rests on VnExpress. No primary text could be found for Mat Velloso's moves (his LinkedIn posts are not admissible), so Brazilian trade reporting (Baguete) is used. Bloomberg, The Information, the Wall Street Journal and Semafor originals were not retrieved (paywalled); the relaying reports are cited instead.
- **nl** ([drafts/nl.json](drafts/nl.json)): Searched (web search for discovery only; never quoted) and retrieved with tools.mjs on 2026-10-29: ssi.inc (home, updates) and the SSI–NVIDIA GlobeNewswire release linked from it; thinkingmachines.ai (home, news, NVIDIA partnership); Mira Murati’s X post of 15 July 2025 on the seed round (syndication returned only the first 280 characters; relay refused); TechCrunch on the TML seed; reflection.ai (home, about, newsroom, blog, “Building Frontier Open Intelligence”) and TechCrunch of 9 October 2025; periodic.com (home, news, “Introducing Periodic Labs”) and TechCrunch of 30 September 2025; Karpathy’s X post of 16 July 2024 and eurekalabs.ai; karpathy.ai (no Eureka Labs mention); ndea.com (home, about) and Chollet’s X post of 15 January 2025; humansand.ai (direct and relay) and TechCrunch of 20 January 2026; appliedcompute.com (home, blog, two company posts) and a TechCrunch list entry for full names; ricursive.com, its newsletter page (empty shell) and two TechCrunch reports. Existing records were checked in existing-claims.txt for every person and organisation; most SSI, TML, Reflection and Periodic founders and roles are already in the dataset and are not redrafted.
- **oa** ([drafts/oa.json](drafts/oa.json)): Searched the existing-claims inventory for every person and organisation named in the brief and found here (Brockman, Karnofsky, Karpathy, Metz, Zoph, Weng, Scandinaro, Kosic, Jumper and others). Web searches (discovery only) for 2023–2026 moves into, out of and between OpenAI and Anthropic: Brockman's 2024 sabbatical and return; Karnofsky at Anthropic; Tim Brooks to Google DeepMind; Logan Kilpatrick to Google; Andrea Vallone to Anthropic; Sam Schoenholz's return; OpenAI researchers to Meta Superintelligence Labs and back (Beyer, Kolesnikov, Zhai, Jason Wei, Hyung Won Chung, Verma, Knight, Metz); Google DeepMind researchers to Anthropic (Carlini, Jumper, Houlsby, Sohl-Dickstein, Anil); 2026 moves (Schwarzer, Karpathy, Tworek, Steinberger, Dean Ball); xAI to OpenAI (Ruddarraju, Liberatore); Microsoft to OpenAI (Bubeck); Sachin Katti; Alec Radford. X posts were read through X's syndication endpoint (which truncates long notes and cannot read image notes); company pages and reports directly or through the reader relay. The relay was intermittently blocked (HTTP 401) for this IP, which defeated Axios, GeekWire and DCD; The Verge and LinkedIn were not attempted as evidence. Primary texts were used wherever retrieved; 15 records rest on reporting and say so.
- **xa** ([drafts/xa.json](drafts/xa.json)): Searched on 2026-10-29 the dataset inventory (existing-claims, existing-entities, people-pack-claims) for xAI, Amazon, Apple and each person and investor named below, and data/claims.json for xAI's capital records (Series B total and Sequoia; Series C total and NVIDIA; Series E total and NVIDIA). Retrieved xAI's Series B, C and E announcements directly from x.ai (which answered direct HTTP this time), HUMAIN's press release on its Series E investment, Andy Jassy's message of 17 December 2025 and the Amazon Nova announcement of 3 December 2024 from aboutamazon.com, Apple's announcement of 1 December 2025, and Robert Keele's X post through the syndication endpoint. Web searches (discovery only) covered xAI executives 2024–2026 (CFOs, general counsel, president, heads of teams, hires from and moves to other frontier labs), Amazon's AGI organisation 2023–2026 (Prasad, DeSantis, Abbeel, Luan, the AGI Lab), and Apple's foundation-model and AI leadership 2025–2026. No primary text was found for the xAI finance and legal appointments, Nicolls's presidency, Ruddarraju's and Kosic's moves, Luan's departure or Ke Yang's departure; those records rest on reporting (TechCrunch, CFO Dive, Fortune, Bloomberg Government, DatacenterDynamics, Reuters via StreetInsider, Next, AppleInsider, 36Kr) and say so. GeekWire refused this environment (Cloudflare block); a Bloomberg article was not retrieved because it is paywalled. xAI's Series C names twelve financial investors besides NVIDIA and AMD; only AMD was drafted (see omitted).

## Held and dropped at integration

Records drafted but left out of the pack by the integrator (`integration.json`): duplicates of another draft or of a dataset record, and records held for a maintainer decision. They stay in their drafts.

| --------------------------------------------- | --------------------------------------------- | ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ |
| `lg2-oa-liberatore-xai-cfo-2025`              | `lg2-xa-liberatore-xai-cfo-2025`              | Same proposition from the same CFO Dive report; the kept record gives both reported months.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    |
| `lg2-oa-liberatore-openai-bfo-2025`           | `lg2-xa-liberatore-openai-bfo-2025`           | Same proposition from the same CFO Dive report.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `lg2-oa-ruddarraju-xai-infra-prior`           | `lg2-xa-ruddarraju-xai-infrastructure-prior`  | Same proposition (his earlier xAI infrastructure role); the kept record rests on DatacenterDynamics, the dropped one on exchange4media; neither gives a start.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `lg2-gm-silver-leaves-gdm-2026`               | `lg2-eu-silver-deepmind-departure-2026`       | Same proposition from the same Fortune report; kept with the Ineffable Intelligence records.                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   |
| `lg2-oa-brockman-openai-sabbatical-2024`      | —                                             | held: a leave is not a departure; no leave predicate exists (open question for the maintainer)                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 |
| `lg2-nl-sutskever-ssi-cofounder-primary`      | `ilya-sutskever-ssi-cofounder-2024`           | Proposed instead as additional primary evidence for the existing dataset record                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                |
| `lg2-dl-cma-microsoft-inflection-assets-2024` | `lg-deal-microsoft-inflection-agreement-2024` | held: an `acquired` edge between the companies would read as an acquisition of Inflection AI; the CMA’s wording is proposed as additional evidence for the existing agreement record (open question)                                                                                                                                                                                                                           |
| `lg2-gm-ramani-msl-infra-lead-2025`           | —                                             | Rests only on BTW Media, an aggregator page with a machine confidence label and a revision dated a year after publication; the one closer report tried (CNBC, 21 August 2025, relaying the Wall Street Journal on the four-team structure) does not name Ramani, and no report of Wang’s memo naming her was retrieved. Her Meta Superintelligence Labs infrastructure role remains in lg2-gm-ramani-msl-vp-engineering-prior. |
